scripted_gui = {
	parliament_gui = {	#党内斗争
		context_type = decision_category
		window_name = "parliament_gui_container"

		dynamic_lists = {
			parliament_main_grid = {
				array = parliament_main_array
				index = parliament_main_index
				change_scope = no
				entry_container = parliament_main_entry
			}
			parliament_semicircle_main_grid = {
				array = parliament_main_array
				index = parliament_main_index
				change_scope = no
				entry_container = parliament_semicircle_main_entry
			}
			parliament_member_grid = {
				array = parliament_member_array
				index = parliament_member_index
				change_scope = no
				entry_container = parliament_member_entry
			}
		}

		
		properties = {
			parliament_main_icon = {
				frame = v
			}
			parliament_semicircle_main_icon = {
				frame = v
				x = parliament_x_array^parliament_main_index
				y = parliament_y_array^parliament_main_index
			}
			parliament_member_icon = {
				frame = v
			}
		}
	}

	# >>> 游击队：势力地图与地块查询
	PRC_g_map_switch = { #游击队势力地图开关
		context_type = player_context
		parent_window_token = top_bar
		window_name = "PRC_g_map_switch_window"
		visible = {
			PRC_g_can_view_map = yes #允许查看游击队势力地图
		}
		effects = {
			map_toggle_click = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = PRC_g_map_open
					}
					clr_country_flag = PRC_g_map_open
					clr_country_flag = PRC_g_query_open
				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = PRC_g_map_open
				}
			}
		}
		ai_enabled = {
			always = no
		}
	}

	PRC_g_state_actions = { #所选地区的游击队行动
		context_type = selected_state_context
		window_name = "PRC_g_state_window"
		visible = {
			ROOT = {
				tag = PRC
			}
			has_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = PRC_g_activity
			}
		}
		effects = {
			actions_toggle_click = {
				PRC = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = PRC_g_actions_collapsed
						}
						clr_country_flag = PRC_g_actions_collapsed
					}
					else = {
						set_country_flag = PRC_g_actions_collapsed
					}
				}
			}
			amass_support_click = {
				PRC_g_begin_support = yes #开始积累民众支持
			}
			uprising_click = {
				PRC_g_uprise = yes #发动地区起义
			}
			attack_click = {
				PRC_g_attack_army = yes #游击队攻击敌军
			}
			warfare_click = {
				PRC_g_wage_war = yes #开展或升级当地游击战
			}
			sabotage_click = {
				PRC_g_sabotage = yes #破坏当地工业与基础设施
			}
		}
		triggers = {
			actions_body_visible = {
				PRC = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = PRC_g_actions_collapsed
					}
				}
			}
			amass_support_click_enabled = {
				PRC_g_can_amass_support = yes #可以积累民众支持
			}
			uprising_click_enabled = {
				PRC_g_can_uprise = yes #可以发动和平地区起义
			}
			attack_click_enabled = {
				PRC_g_can_attack = yes #可以攻击当地敌军
			}
			warfare_click_enabled = {
				PRC_g_can_wage_war = yes #可以开展当地游击战
			}
			sabotage_click_enabled = {
				PRC_g_can_sabotage = yes #可以破坏当地工业
			}
		}
		ai_test_scopes = test_neighbouring_states
		ai_test_scopes = test_enemy_controlled_states
		ai_test_scopes = test_ally_controlled_states
		ai_test_interval = 25
		ai_test_variance = 0.1
		ai_enabled = {
			tag = PRC
		}
		ai_weights = {
			amass_support_click = {
				ai_will_do = {
					factor = 200
				}
			}
			uprising_click = {
				ai_will_do = {
					factor = 200
				}
			}
			attack_click = {
				ai_will_do = {
					factor = 100
				}
			}
			warfare_click = {
				ai_will_do = {
					factor = 50
				}
			}
			sabotage_click = {
				ai_will_do = {
					factor = 50
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	PRC_g_query_view = { #地块查询独立窗口与关闭事件
		context_type = player_context
		parent_scripted_gui = "PRC_g_map_view"
		window_name = "PRC_g_query_window"
		visible = {
			PRC_g_can_view_map = yes #允许查看游击队势力地图
			has_country_flag = PRC_g_map_open
			has_country_flag = PRC_g_query_open
		}
		effects = {
			query_close_click = { #关闭地块查询
				clr_country_flag = PRC_g_query_open
			}
		}
		ai_enabled = {
			always = no
		}
	}
	# <<< 游击队：势力地图与地块查询

}
